## K3 membership value column (post-deploy admissions and re-scans) — PASS
- kind: sql
- command: `uv run cobalt db query --side system --prod --format json 'WITH scan AS ( SELECT max(last_scan_id) AS id FROM system.radar_pool WHERE pool_key = '"'"'primary'"'"' AND last_scan_at >= TIMESTAMPTZ '"'"'2026-10-14T19:39:41-04:00'"'"'), admitted AS ( SELECT m.rank_metric, m.rank_value, m.last_rank, m.first_seen_at >= TIMESTAMPTZ '"'"'2026-10-14T19:39:41-04:00'"'"' AS inserted_after, (m.left_at IS NULL AND m.last_scan_id = scan.id) AS rescanned_after FROM system.radar_membership m CROSS JOIN scan WHERE m.entered_at IS NOT NULL) SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE inserted_after) AS post_deploy_admitted, count(*) FILTER (WHERE inserted_after AND rank_metric IS NULL AND last_rank IS NOT NULL) AS metric_missing, count(*) FILTER (WHERE inserted_after AND rank_value IS NULL) AS value_null, count(*) FILTER (WHERE inserted_after AND rank_metric IS NULL AND last_rank IS NULL) AS unranked_retained, count(*) FILTER (WHERE rescanned_after) AS rescanned_admitted, count(*) FILTER (WHERE rescanned_after AND rank_metric IS NULL AND last_rank IS NOT NULL) AS rescanned_metric_missing FROM admitted'`
- expected: metric_missing = 0 over admitted rows FIRST SEEN at/after the cutoff that the scan RANKED (last_rank not null) yet stored no rank_metric AND rescanned_metric_missing = 0 over open admitted rows the pool's latest post-cutoff scan touched, counted the same way (the RETAIN path, where a dropped metric would otherwise hide behind a pre-deploy first_seen_at). Both admitted counts 0 → KNOWN. A sticky-retained member the scan no longer ranks stores last_rank, rank_metric, rank_value = NULL, NULL, NULL by design (radar/pool.py:50-54, Transition: "None where no ranking happened"), so it is NOT graded: it is printed as unranked_retained. Only a write-path defect fails K3, not a designed NULL (plan-s2-p4-2026-09-15.md:243, Astra R1-19 / R1-24). value_null and unranked_retained are evidence only: a genuinely absent metric stores rank_value NULL. ONE KNOWN AMBIGUITY in the second set: a frozen HOLD stamps last_scan_id too (radar/store.py's COALESCE branch), so a pre-deploy episode HELD this scan because its only source was degraded carries its pre-deploy NULL into this count, and no column tells it apart from a RETAIN defect. A rescanned_metric_missing FAIL is read against radar_pool.degraded_sources before it is ruled known.
- verdict detail: metric_missing ok; rescanned_metric_missing ok
```

0	0	0	0	50	0
```
